ParlaMint-GR_2019-01-23-S1-commons.u185	The Group of the People's League - Golden Dawn Parliament has raised objections of unconstitutionality and with very specific arguments I will prove - a letter, a word - a paragraph that this agreement violates the law, is in breach of the Greek Constitution. I am going specifically to the articles of the Constitution that are violated by the Ambassadors' Agreement. As far as the use of the term "Macedonia" by Skopje, as regards the use of the name "Region of the Hellenic Republic" is concerned, Articles 101 and 102 of the Greek Constitution are violated here. In particular, this article of the Prespecies Agreement contradicts the substance of the Constitution, i.e. substantive provisions and, in particular, the provisions on the organisation and decentralisation of the Greek state, since the neighbouring state manifestly shows that the term "Macedonia", which is officially used in three secondary local government agencies, that is, regions of Greece, by corresponding geographical identification, namely the Region of Central, Western and Eastern Macedonia. Therefore, in the light of the foregoing, this draft law circumvents the substance of the Constitution and is an issue of substantial unconstitutionality and infringement at the outset of Articles 101 and 102 of the Constitution. Second case of violation of articles in the Constitution: Mr Nimic appears as witness in the Ambassadors' Agreement. Mr Nimic represents a specific international organisation, the United Nations. He is a personal envoy of the Secretary-General of the United Nations. The Prespecies Agreement therefore violates Article 28(2) of the Constitution, since this is the case in which it is recognised to Matthew Nimic that the competence provided for by the Constitution of Greece is to do so in the abovementioned respects and in the present and future defence of the integrity of the country which is at risk by conferring use of the name of Macedonia. The Government in this agreement, which resulted in irregularities and illegal acts, serves - it says - a great national interest and promotes the cooperation of the Hellenic Republic with the second part. We therefore have the definition of a violation of Article 28(2) of the Constitution. Article 4 of the condemnation of altruism and the prohibition of the future amendment of the constitution. Article 6(3) condemns chauvinism, hostility, alutism. This is the provision that excludes any interference in our internals and evacuates once and for all the claim of a 'Macedonian' minority. They did so and the constitutional review completed it. However, particularly for the European Union, Article 2(4) expressly states in paragraph 1 that the only obligation we undertake is to support the opening of negotiations, not its next steps. And that is one of the additional weapons that the agreement gives us, if there is any interference from it, to be able to insist on its faithful implementation, through the veto that we will have in the European Union. We must stop, ladies and gentlemen, the fear of our country. Our country in the Balkans can play a leading role not imperialist, as the Communist Party of Greece says, but a role of peace and co-development. It is dreadfully hypocritical and irrational, to think that it is a danger to a country that has a seventeen-fold smaller economy than our own and virtually non-existent military means. Based on the above resolution on Macedonian language, the Federal Yugoslav Republic of Macedonia became active in international organisations, particularly the International Standardisation Organisation, in which Greece has for many years been reaping to participate, and even had the right to vote. In 1977 Greece did not express any reservations about the Macedonian language and Macedonian mainland characters or about the right of the then Federal Yugoslav Republic of Macedonia to act as an official geographical designation body. In 1982 Greece confirmed from the UN step - to take a little notice of this - that despite the long historical use of Greek sites, in areas outside the limits of the current Greek state, it supports the abolition as quickly as possible of the exonerations in international use and respect for the national sites, as formulated by the relevant Geographical Names Authority of each country, namely the Federal Yugoslav Republic at that time. Greece did not object - because it was lacking - to introduce MK/MKD abbreviations into the international standard ISO 3166/1993, nor to introduce MK/MKD and MAC abbreviations - because it was lacking in international standards ISO 639. Aggression in foreign policy easily turns into aggression within, into inclusive introversion and isolationism, into indifference about what education and culture can teach us. The contempt and blind enforcement mix to neighbors is easily transformed into a crisis of majesty and arrogance, not only to the outsiders, but also to them within. We are celebrating the two hundred years since Greek independence and Greece has made a huge contribution to the introduction of this principle in the world. You see, Skopje can be called "North Macedonia", however, with the tolerance or consent of the Greek Government, their people are identified as "Macedonian" and not as "North-Macedonian". This particular pursuit of the Macedonians has not been accepted in the past by any Greek government. Nor did any Greek government ever accept the concrete pursuit of Skopje in the past. Seven former Prime Ministers were vertically opposed to the above issues: Konstantinos Karamanlis, Andreas Papandreou, Kostas Mitsotakis, Costas Simitis, Kostas Karamanlis, Antonis Samaras, George Papandreou. They were all opposite! The dangerous part of our recognition of a so-called Macedonian language is that, with the ratification of the Prespecies Agreement, this language, without any determination, will also have effect in Greek law. I shall read to you Article 1(8) of the Agreement: ‘From the entry into force of this Agreement, the Parties shall use the name and terminologies of Article 1(3) for all uses and for all purposes of erga omnes’, i.e. internally, in all their bilateral relations and in all regional and international organisations and institutions. The paragraph I have just read to you opens the way for the neighbours to speak of an alleged Macedonian language in Greece and thus for a so-called Macedonian minority in Greece, a Macedonian minority that will then put us and tell us that it has rights. The acidification, of course, is - and I would like to give another dimension to the problem of relations between the two countries - that there is no reference in the Prespecies Agreement to the Greek minority living in Skopje. Its population size may be indiscreet, but I would like to stress that in 1991 Bulgaria had submitted a memorandum to the OSCE, claiming that the state of Skopje was inhabited by two hundred thousand Greeks. The only official element in the state is the 1951 census that recorded a hundred and fifty thousand Greeks in Skopje. They are mainly veilers of Greeks who are afraid to manifest themselves and for whom the Greek state must and must be interested. Of these 100 thousand were vegetarian Greeks - "Grek", as they were called and called in the state - twenty-five thousand were Greek native inhabitants of the Monastery, three thousand Greek Saraksanese and about thirty thousand political refugees. Totally, in 1951 they accounted for 18% of the then Skopje population, which did not exceed nine hundred thousand inhabitants. To these people we could give Greek citizenship or citizenship as the Bulgarians did for their peers, exercising a different policy. We could also hug them, showing them they're not alone. We could keep their Greek national conscience firm. To sum up and close, for me North Macedonia is Serres, it's Drama, it's Edessa, it's Veria, it's Kilkis. Over Doirani, over Prespes, Skopje is Vardharska, it's Dardania or whatever they want to call themselves. It is certainly not Macedonia, because 'Macedonia is one and it is Greek'. That is why I am looking at you, ladies and gentlemen, in the eyes now that you are about to sell out the place that we live on, asking you to stand up to your conscience. History neither shares nor rejoices. Macedonia does not belong to you, nor is it a merchandise to sell it. Macedonia has a soul, a history, a struggle and hundreds of thousands of dead who have been sacrificed for its liberation. In this national crime, New Democracy cannot become an accomplice. This is not allowed by the principles and values of any one of those who are our parliamentary group. And because you often refer to the good neighborhood, I glorify God that in 1821 Theodore Kolokotronis and the rest of the heroes of the 1821 National Liberation Struggle have not been reconciled with Ibrahim and Koutakhi in the name of the then good neighborhood. Thank you.

The distribution of geographical Macedonia between the Balkan states was finalised with the Treaty of Bucharest in 1913, under which the Balkan Wars ended. None of the Parties will allow its territory to be used against the other Party by any third State.
